# Comments to decide on before this repository goes public

Generated by `scripts/collect_comment_flags.py`. **Nothing here has been changed** -- this is a review list, not a diff.

`dasm2`'s comments are unusually detailed, and a lot of that detail is about work that is not this paper. Three rules for working through the list:

- **Generic explanation stays.** An explanation of why the code does what it does is the reason this tree is readable. Deleting it to be safe would make this repository worse than the one it came from.
- **A private issue reference goes or gets inlined.** `#1234` is a dead link for every reader of this repository. If the comment is only a pointer, cut it; if the issue holds the reason, write the reason into the comment.
- **Another project's result goes.** A measurement, a benchmark or a design argument from antibody, BCR, ProteinGym, Tsuboyama, Compara, QDASM or ESM2 work is the part that actually leaks. The dead link is the small problem.

## Totals

- 1235 flagged lines across 157 of 218 files
- 905 private issue references, 141 distinct issues

Most-cited issues: #621 (64), #724 (51), #1220 (39), #747 (29), #1238 (24), #199 (24), #1272 (23), #513 (23), #1039 (22), #525 (21)

```python
    accumulating. Measured on the tang checkpoint at 32,768 PCPs (issue #988),
    none of these rise: RSS moved 0.19 MB/chunk over 256 chunks and was flat
    after the first ~30, which is why this loop has no periodic trim.
```
